## ~~~~Devastation Evoker Overview

Do you like dragons? Have you ever wondered what it would be like to fly above your enemies while raining down volcanic death from above? Do you like the idea of playing a ranged caster that feels like more of a Battlemage with flashy spells? **Devastation Evoker might be the specialization for you!**

_Your awakening was unprecedented, but the time has come to claim your place in the world of Azeroth._

_The Dragon Isles are waiting for you to discover your purpose and awaken your wing mates in your new adventures. You will not be so easily contained._

Dracthyr Evokers are elite soldiers that combine both the essence of dragons and the adaptability of mortal races to wield the magic of **all five dragonflights**. Devastation is a ranged DPS specialization focused on using attacks with the explosive power of the Red Dragonflight, as well as the focused and overwhelming magic of the Blue Dragonflight, to harm foes from a distance. Devastation Evokers unleash a variety of spells, including classic dragon breaths, from mid-range. The color of their effects categorizes these spells.

## Devastation Evoker Strengths and Weaknesses

|     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **Strengths** |  | **Weaknesses** |
| - Strong AoE and priority damage on new targets.<br>  <br>- Unique utility spells.<br>  <br>- Strong two-target cleave.<br>  <br>- Spread cleave up to 25 yards.<br>  <br>- Decent survivability with armor to mitigate Physical damage.<br>  <br>- Excellent mobility and the ability to control of your character when falling with [Glide](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358733).<br>  <br>- Provides off-healing as a DPS specialization, with an incentive to throw in healing spells sporadically by providing damage with [Scarlet Adaptation](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=372469) and [Ancient Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369990).<br>  <br>- Dragon (this one speaks for itself). |  | - Limited to a 25 yard maximum range as a ranged caster.<br>  <br>- Extremely high threat output (i.e. easier to get enemy aggro), with no way to mitigate it.<br>  <br>- No immunity. Defensives need to be used _before_ the damage is done.<br>  <br>- Shares the same class as Augmentation, which is a specialization that sometimes overshadows other DPS. This is only a weakness if you don't enjoy the support playstyle of Augmentation and don't want to be asked to play it.<br>  <br>- Transmog options are extremely limited. |

## ~~~~ Devastation Evoker Changes in Midnight

With Midnight, WoW's core combat systems have received substantial changes. Similar to most other specs, Devastation has received a few key changes that impact how the spec feels to play.

### Removed Abilities

Devastation lost the following abilities in Midnight:

- **[Shattering Star](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1265802)** \- This talent now adds a passive effect to [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge).

- **[Firestorm](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369846)** \- This talent now adds a passive effect to [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre).

- **[Renewing Blaze](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=374348)** \- This talent now adds a passive effect to [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_artifact_dragonscales.gif)Obsidian Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=363916/obsidian-scales).

- **[Unravel](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1264378)** \- This talent now adds a passive effect to [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath).


### New Abilities

Devastation gains the following abilities. Major effects through new Apex Talents have also been added:

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_10_skinning_dragonscales_blue.gif)Azure Sweep](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1265867/azure-sweep) is a new enhanced [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_azurestrike.gif)Azure Strike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362969/azure-strike)

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv12_apextalent_evoker_risingfury.gif)Rising Fury](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1271687/rising-fury) (Apex Talent)

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv12_apextalent_evoker_risingfury.gif)Rising Fury](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1271796/rising-fury) (Apex Talent)

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv12_apextalent_evoker_risingfury.gif)Rising Fury](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1271788/rising-fury) (Apex Talent)


### Big Changes

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) can now be cast while casting.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_blackattunementalt.gif)Strafing Run](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1266151/strafing-run) allows for more [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) casts.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_misc_volatileair.gif)Twin Guardian](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370888/twin-guardian) now provides a short burst of speed and cast-while-moving instead of a shield.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) extensions are are uncapped, but diminish with each extension.


### Hero Talents

- Flameshaper \- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_misc_herb_cinderbloom_petal.gif)Lifecinders](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=444322/lifecinders) now grants allies a weakened [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_artifact_dragonscales.gif)Obsidian Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=363916/obsidian-scales)

- Flameshaper \- Engulf replaced with two charges of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath).

  - Fire Breath damage over time is converted into passive explosions with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_shadowflames_wave.gif)Consume Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=444088/consume-flame)


- Scalecommander enhanced with talents like [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_dragonriding_dynamicflight01.gif)Command Squadron](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1260745/command-squadron) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_10_dungeonjewelry_dragon_trinket_1arcanemagical_blue.gif)Concentrated Power](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1261448/concentrated-power).

  - [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_dragonriding_barrelroll01.gif)Slipstream](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441257/slipstream) only refunds one [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover), but you get twice the casts.


## Devastation Evoker Hero Talents: Scalecommander and Flameshaper

For Hero Talents, Devastation Evokers have to pick between Scalecommander and Flameshaper.

- Scalecommander gain access to improved versions of existing spells like [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Maneuverability](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=433871/maneuverability), while barraging enemies with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_ability_scalecommanderevoker_bombardments.gif)Bombardments](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=434300/bombardments).

- Flameshaper has enhanced [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath), which serves as fuel for powerful [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_shadowflames_wave.gif)Consume Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=444088/consume-flame) explosions.


Scalecommander is recommended in all content. Flameshaper brings higher burst, but Scalecommander has superior damage overall and spread cleave.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/medium/ability_marksmanship.jpg)Talent Builds](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/talent-builds-pve-dps)

## ~~~~ Playing an Devastation Evoker in Midnight Season 1

For more details on how to play Devastation, you can check out our [rotation guide](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/rotation-cooldowns-pve-dps).

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| |     |     |
| --- | --- |
| ~~~~ | **Build and Spend:** [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) spend [Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/resource/essence-19), primary resource for Evokers. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_azurestrike.gif)Azure Strike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362969/azure-strike) are the filler spells that generate [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_essenceburst.gif)Essence Burst](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359618/essence-burst), a proc that allows you to use these abilities without spending Essence. Spend Essence and Essence Burst, then use filler spells while waiting. |

* * *

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| ~~~~ | **Use cooldowns frequently:** [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) (Scalecommander) are Devastation's main cooldown. Essence Burst is guaranteed from filler spells during this buff. Its duration can be extended by both [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_nature_shamanrage.gif)Animosity](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375797/animosity). Given that both spells also come with their own short cooldowns, consider what is available when entering Dragonrage and to press them on cooldown during it. |

* * *

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| ~~~~ | **Always be casting:** Try to maintain high uptime on targets. Constant use of damaging spells is the key to high damage in Raid and Mythic+. Use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) effectively to cast spells on the move. Try to fill "downtime" in your rotation with spells, even if its not completely optimal (Azure Strike for instance). Using _something_ is almost always better than being idle. Try to use cooldowns and Empowered spells early and often. | |

## ~~~~ Gearing an Devastation Evoker in Midnight Season 1

As a rough guideline, follow these general stat priorities when gearing your Devastation Evoker:

- **Intellect >> Crit > Haste = Mastery > Versatility**


Don't neglect the value of **Intellect**, as a substantial increase in item level is generally worth it compared to optimized stats. It is always recommended that you use [Raidbots](https://www.raidbots.com/simbot/topgear) to sim your own character since every character has different circumstances. Also, check out our guide to the [Great Vault](https://www.wowhead.com/guide=17592) to learn how to increase the chances of obtaining BiS gear for your Devastation Evoker.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/medium/inv_chest_chain_03.jpg)Best-in-Slot Gear](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/bis-gear)

## ~~~~ What Professions are Best for Devastation Evoker?

Compared to previous expansions, professions in Midnight provide minimal benefits towards the performance of your spec. This means that Devastation Evoker players can choose any profession according to personal preference. You might consider [Leatherworking](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/midnight/professions/leatherworking-overview-trainer-locations-recipes-tools) to craft your own armor, as many profession items can be very close to your [Best in Slot gear](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/bis-gear). On the other hand, you may opt for [Alchemy](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/midnight/professions/alchemy-overview-trainer-locations-recipes-tools) to craft consumables or [Enchanting](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/midnight/professions/enchanting-overview-trainer-locations-recipes-tools) to enchant gear without relying on the Auction House.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/medium/inv_inscription_80_warscroll_intellect.jpg)Stats](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/stat-priority-pve-dps)

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/medium/ui_concentration.jpg)Professions in Midnight](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/professions/overview)

## ~~~~ What Race is Best for Devastation Evoker?

Devastation Evoker can only be Dracthyr for both Horde and Alliance.

## Preheat

### Coach, Former RWF Raider, & World of Warcraft Retail Expert

[Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/Preheat)[Discord](https://discord.gg/gd7jEPVcAT)[YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/@Preheat)[Bluesky](https://bsky.app/profile/preheat.bsky.social)[TikTok](https://www.tiktok.com/@preheatwow)[Author Page](https://www.wowhead.com/author/Preheat)

Preheat has raided at the highest level in the game for many years, was a founding member of LIMIT (now [Liquid](https://raider.io/guilds/us/illidan/Liquid)) and was an officer when they got World First. He also participated in the 2nd MDI back in Legion and was Rank 1 that season. He has also commentated multiple community events such as the [Keystone Masters](https://twitter.com/keystonemasters), numerous charity events, and the [Team Liquid](https://twitter.com/TeamLiquid) Race to World First. For the last 3 years, he has helped over 240 players improve their gameplay through [one-on-one coaching](https://metafy.gg/@preheat/sessions).

These days, Preheat focuses on content creation, casting events like the Race to World first, making guides, and coaching. Sharing what he knows and helping others improve is a passion of his and it is a big part of what drives him to keep playing the game.

Outside of World of Warcraft, Preheat has worked in the real world as a software trainer and has almost a decade of experience teaching people, writing documentation, and creating lesson plans for those looking to learn new, technical things.

For all things Evoker and Mage, you can find him in his [Discord](https://discord.gg/HzCKceX), on [Twitter](https://twitter.com/LimitPreheat), posting videos to [YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/c/preheat), and streaming on [Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat).

Now the Dragonflight expansion has come to an end it is open to fair reviews, let me start by saying the class is not as chaotic as it may seem at first glance. The mobility is comparable to that of its predecessor the Demon Hunter and it is difficult to not love the animation of **Deep Breath** and **Earthquake** in both mythics, raids and player vs player content. After levelling as both Devastation and Preservation I found myself wanting to play the DPS specialization more and more and absolutely loving the numbers during dungeons and mythics. Even while under-geared at level 70 the AoE output is incredible with Fire Breath, Pyre and Firestorm procs, the single target damage isn't too bad either and if talented into Eternity Surge is absolutely ridiculous. A fully geared Devastation will always be welcomed in any raid I'm sure.

# Devastation Evoker Stat Priority - Midnight

[By Preheat](https://www.wowhead.com/author/Preheat)
Updated:
2026/03/14

LivePreheat[Live on Twitch!](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat) Offline on TwitchFollow: Follow [Discord](https://discord.gg/SmWHj8cQp7) [Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat) [Twitter](https://twitter.com/LimitPreheat) [YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/@Preheat)

Stats are a key component of customizing your Devastation Evoker in Midnight. Having the right distribution of stats on your gear can be critical towards optimizing your performance.

This guide details the best stat priority for your Devastation Evoker, as well as covering how to determine Devastation Evoker stat priorities personalized for your character in **Midnight Season 1**, as well as how to check whether an item Best-in-Slot, an upgrade, or outright bad.

It also covers Devastation Evoker's preferred stats in-depth, accounting for nuances and synergies for niche situations that go beyond a generic priority.

[Overview](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/overview-pve-dps) [BiS Gear](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/bis-gear) [Rotation](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/rotation-cooldowns-pve-dps) [Talent Builds](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/talent-builds-pve-dps) [Consumables](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/enchants-gems-pve-dps) [Stats](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/stat-priority-pve-dps) [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/large/inv_misc_groupneedmore.jpg)\\
Basics](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/basics)

Show Table of Contents

## ~~~~ Best Stats for Devastation Evoker

Devastation Evoker stats depend on the hero talent, but both Flameshaper and Scalecommander want **Critical Strike**.Scalecommander has added **Mastery** value (due to [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath)) and **Haste** is strong for Devastation in general.

All Secondary stats start to diminish if you continue stacking them, so usually you end up with a more even spread as your item level increases. It is always recommended that you use [Raidbots](https://www.raidbots.com/simbot/stats) to sim your own character, since every character is different.

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Flameshaper Stat Priority**<br>* * *<br>1. **Intellect**<br>   <br>2. **Critical Strike**<br>   <br>3. **Haste**<br>   <br>4. **Mastery**<br>   <br>5. **Versatility** | **Scalecommander Stat Priority**<br>* * *<br>1. **Intellect**<br>   <br>2. **Critical Strike**<br>   <br>3. **Haste**<br>   <br>4. **Mastery**<br>   <br>5. **Versatility** |

While not unlimited, gearing provides considerable freedom when customizing stats. Following the suggestions below, we've tailored recommendations of best-in-slot gear to seek during **Midnight Season 1** for Devastation Evoker in our Gearing Guide.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/medium/inv_chest_chain_03.jpg)Devastation Evoker Gearing Guide](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/bis-gear)

## ~~~~ Stats Explained

**Intellect** is the primary stat for Devastation Evoker, and you'll find it on most of your gear, outside of Rings, Neck, and some Trinkets.

For Secondary stats, Devastation Evoker gains the following from each stat:

- **Critical Strike**: Chance for attacks and heals to deal double damage. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_shaman_shockinglava.gif)Spellweaver's Dominance](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370845/spellweavers-dominance) takes this even further.

- **Haste**: Decreases the cast time of your spells (including Empowered spells), adds ticks to our DoT effects (such as [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath)), and reduces the Global Cooldown (GCD).

- **Mastery**: [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_masterygiantkiller.gif)Mastery: Giantkiller](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362980/mastery-giantkiller) increases damage to targets, based on their health percentage.Additional Notes

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_masterygiantkiller.gif)Mastery: Giantkiller](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362980/mastery-giantkiller) scales down linearly, which means that it is at full value when the target is at 100% health and half value when the target is at 50%. At 30% health and below, the bonus acts as if the target still has 30% all the way until it dies. This means 30% is the "floor" for Mastery. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2.gif)Tyranny](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=376888/tyranny) give the full value of Mastery, regardless of the target's health.

- **Versatility**: Increases personal damage and healing, while also reducing damage taken.


## ~~~~ Sim Your Devastation Evoker

The suggestions for stat priorities mentioned above are generic; exact values will change based on your gear. To gain a proper understanding of your optimal stats, we recommend **simming your character** using various tools available in the community, such as [SimulationCraft](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/how-to-use-simulationcraft-and-pawn-4885). You can easily sim your character and identify your stat priority with Raidbots, an online platform! Explore more about Raidbots in our [comprehensive guide](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/how-to-use-raidbots-and-run-character-simulations-6050).

## ~~~~ Diminishing Returns in Midnight

Accumulating too much of one Secondary stat from your gear leads to diminishing returns. This means that adding more of the same stat beyond certain thresholds results in reduced benefits. These thresholds are fixed and reduce the amount gained from more of that stat rating by 10% per breakpoint. Each stat has a different rating threshold where the reduction begins:

- **Critical Strike** \- Above 1380 rating

- **Haste** \- Above 1320 rating

- **Mastery** \- Above 1380 rating

- **Versatility** \- Above 1620 rating


**Example**

Let's assume you gain 1% Haste for 10 Haste Rating. When above the diminishing return breakpoint, 10 Haste Rating would give you 0.9% Haste instead, resulting in a 10% reduction from what you gained previously.

Note that this system **only counts stat rating**, meaning that percentage-based procs and effects like [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_nature_bloodlust.gif)Bloodlust](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=2825/bloodlust) aren't factored in and will always grant their full benefit.

You can learn more about stat diminishing returns in our detailed guide:

[What are Stat Diminishing Returns](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/diminishing-returns-on-secondary-stats-in-world-of-warcraft)

# Devastation Evoker Abilities and Talents Guide - Midnight

[By Preheat](https://www.wowhead.com/author/Preheat)
Updated:
2026/02/25

LivePreheat[Live on Twitch!](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat) Offline on TwitchFollow: Follow [Discord](https://discord.gg/SmWHj8cQp7) [Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat) [Twitter](https://twitter.com/LimitPreheat) [YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/@Preheat)

Explore the depths of the Devastation Evoker talent tree, which contains the bulk of your abilities and customization options. In this Devastation Evoker guide, we review all the different abilities and talents in **Midnight Season 1**, including an explanation for what they do and how to use them correctly.

[Overview](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/overview-pve-dps) [BiS Gear](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/bis-gear) [Rotation](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/rotation-cooldowns-pve-dps) [Talent Builds](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/talent-builds-pve-dps) [Consumables](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/enchants-gems-pve-dps) [Stats](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/stat-priority-pve-dps) [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/large/inv_misc_groupneedmore.jpg)\\
Basics](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/basics)

## ~~~~ Baseline Devastation Evoker Spells

### Offensive Abilities

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_azurestrike.gif)Azure Strike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362969/azure-strike) ( **AS**): Filler ability. Hurl magic at enemies to instantly cleave multiple targets.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) ( **LF**): Filler ability with a twist. Send a bolt of flame to damage an enemy **or** heal an ally. You can cast this on yourself as a heal.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate): [Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/resource/essence-19) Spender. Channel a beam on the target, dealing damage over time and slowing them.


### Offensive Cooldowns

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) ( **FB**): Empower your breath and release a burning cone that deals damage and applies a damage over time (DoT) effect to enemies. Empowering this ability increases the upfront damage at the cost of duration on the DoT.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_furyoftheaspects.gif)Fury of the Aspects](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=390386/fury-of-the-aspects): This is the Evoker version of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_nature_bloodlust.gif)Bloodlust](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=2825/bloodlust). It increases the Haste of all allies for a time.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath): Remove all root effects and become immune to Crowd Control (CC) as you decimate enemies in your path with Volcanic (i.e. a combination of Fire and Nature damage) flame.


### Defensive/Healing Cooldowns

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_emeraldblossom.gif)Emerald Blossom](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=355913/emerald-blossom): AoE Healing on a short cooldown. Place a bulb that blossoms into a heal for injured allies.


### Utility Abilities

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover): Movement ability. Launch yourself in a direction and gain the ability to cast while moving for a time. If used without directional input, you hover in place, and this effect can be used while casting other spells.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_blessingofthebronze.gif)Blessing of the Bronze](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=364342/blessing-of-the-bronze): Raid buff. This spell decreases the cooldown of your allies movement ability. The affected ability [depends on the class](https://www.wowhead.com/news/new-evoker-raid-buff-in-dragonflight-blessing-of-the-bronze-327698). For Evoker, it gives [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) a shorter cooldown.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_return.gif)Return](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361227/return): Bring an ally back to life. Only available outside of combat.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_tailswipe.gif)Tail Swipe](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=368970/tail-swipe): Evoker-specific Racial ability. Swing your tail and knock enemies around you into the air.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_wingbuffet.gif)Wing Buffet](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357214/wing-buffet): Dracthyr Racial ability. Flap your wings to knock away enemies in front of you.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_glide.gif)Glide](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358733/glide): Dracthyr Racial ability. Slow your falling speed and gain control of your momentum.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_soar.gif)Soar](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=381322/soar): Dracthyr Racial ability. Take to the skies and gain Dragonriding controls.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_visage.gif)Visage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=372014/visage): Dracthyr Racial ability. Assume your Visage form, significantly increasing your out of combat health regeneration and showing off your transmog.


### Passive Abilities

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_masterygiantkiller.gif)Mastery: Giantkiller](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362980/mastery-giantkiller) \- Causes you to deal increased damage to enemies, depending on their current Health percentage. The higher their Health, the more damage you deal. Think of this as reverse [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_sword_48.gif)Execute](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=163201/execute).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_nourishingsands.gif)Nourishing Sands](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=406041/nourishing-sands): Dealing a killing blow on an enemy target resets the cooldown and increases the potency of your next [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_emeraldblossom.gif)Emerald Blossom](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=355913/emerald-blossom).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_misc_orb_05.gif)Energizing Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=400006/energizing-flame): Casting [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) as an offensive ability refunds a portion of its Mana cost.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_misc_scales_dragonblue03.gif)Tempered Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=396571/tempered-scales): Your scales increases your armor by 75%. This makes Evokers take less physical damage.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_frost_coldhearted.gif)Permeating Chill](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=381773/permeating-chill) \- Damaging Blue spells reduce your target's movement speed for a brief time.

### Scalecommander Devastation Evoker Talents

**Key Talent**

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate) ( **Mass**) \- After casting [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) or [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge), your [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) now cleaves up to 3 targets. If striking fewer than 3 targets, the damage of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) is increased by for each missing target.

Also applies a DoT to enemies hit.


**Passives**

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_blackattunementalt.gif)Might of the Black Dragonflight](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441705/might-of-the-black-dragonflight): Your Black spells deal more damage.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_10_gearupgrade_drakesshadowflameenhancedcrest.gif)Melt Armor](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441176/melt-armor): [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) causes enemies to take more damage from your [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_ability_scalecommanderevoker_bombardments.gif)Bombardments](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=434300/bombardments) and [Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/resource/essence-19) abilities.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_ability_scalecommanderevoker_bombardments.gif)Bombardments](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=434300/bombardments): [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate) marks your primary target, causing allies' attacks against that target to have a chance to deal additional damage, split among nearby enemies.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_soar.gif)Wingleader](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441206/wingleader): [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_ability_scalecommanderevoker_bombardments.gif)Bombardments](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=434300/bombardments) slightly reduces the cooldown of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) for each target hit.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Onslaught](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441245/onslaught): Gain a stack of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Burnout](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375801/burnout) when you enter combat.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_siege_engineer_superheated.gif)Unrelenting Siege](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441246/unrelenting-siege): [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_azurestrike.gif)Azure Strike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362969/azure-strike), [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame), and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) deal additional damage the longer you are in combat.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_dragonriding_dynamicflight01.gif)Command Squadron](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1260745/command-squadron): While flying in [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath), two Dracthyr Commandos cast up to 4 [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1236970/pyre) s each at enemy targets.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_essenceburststacks.gif)Refined Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1261452/refined-essence): [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate) deals 15% more damage.

## ~~~~ Devastation Evoker Talents

### Evoker Class Talents

#### Row 1 Talents

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_earthen_pillar.gif)Landslide](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358385/landslide): Sends out a path of stone that roots enemies. Damage can break this effect.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_artifact_dragonscales.gif)Obsidian Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=363916/obsidian-scales) **(Scales)**: Defensive cooldown that reduces your damage taken for a short time.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_fontofmagic_green.gif)Expunge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=365585/expunge) **(Dispel)**: Removes a Poison debuff from an ally.


#### Row 2 Talents

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_frost_frostblast.gif)Natural Convergence](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369913/natural-convergence): Passively makes [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) channel faster and reduces the cast time of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_wingbuffet.gif)Heavy Wingbeats](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=368838/heavy-wingbeats): Reduces the cooldown of your racial ability, [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_wingbuffet.gif)Wing Buffet](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357214/wing-buffet).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_rescue.gif)Verdant Embrace](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=360995/verdant-embrace) ( **VE**): Healing and mobility cooldown. Fly to an ally and heal them, or heal yourself instantly.


#### Row 3 Talents

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_earthen_pillar.gif)Forger of Mountains](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375528/forger-of-mountains): Cooldown reduction and buff for [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_earthen_pillar.gif)Landslide](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358385/landslide) that makes it harder to break.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_innatemagic4.gif)Innate Magic](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375520/innate-magic): [Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/resource/essence-19) recharges slightly faster.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_shield_1h_revenantfire_d_01.gif)Obsidian Bulwark](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375406/obsidian-bulwark): [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_artifact_dragonscales.gif)Obsidian Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=363916/obsidian-scales) gains an additional charge.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Enkindled](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375554/enkindled): Increases the healing and damage of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_bijou_red.gif)Scarlet Adaptation](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=372469/scarlet-adaptation): Passively stores effective healing to increase the damage of your next [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame).


#### Row 4 Talents

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_holy_spellwarding.gif)Inner Radiance](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=386405/inner-radiance): The healing effectiveness of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_emeraldblossom.gif)Emerald Blossom](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=355913/emerald-blossom) are increased when used on yourself.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_recall.gif)Recall](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=371806/recall): Allows you to return to where you were when you used [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath), provided you use it within 3 seconds of landing. This ability incurs a global cooldown.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_tailswipe.gif)Clobbering Sweep](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375443/clobbering-sweep): Reduces the cooldown of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_tailswipe.gif)Tail Swipe](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=368970/tail-swipe).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_skyreach_wind.gif)Tailwind](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375556/tailwind): Increases your speed for a brief time when using [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_fontofmagic_red.gif)Cauterizing Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=374251/cauterizing-flame) ( **Caut**): Removes all Bleed, Poison, Curse, and Disease effects from an ally and heal them.


#### Row 5 Talents

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_elemental_mote_fire01.gif)Ancient Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369990/ancient-flame): Healing with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_emeraldblossom.gif)Emerald Blossom](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=355913/emerald-blossom) or [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_rescue.gif)Verdant Embrace](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=360995/verdant-embrace) speeds up your next [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) cast.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_arcane_studentofmagic.gif)Instinctive Arcana](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=376164/instinctive-arcana): A passive increase to all magic damage you deal.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales) ( **Tip**): Cooldown that causes your next [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) or [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) to be instant and fully Empowered.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_rogue_imrovedrecuperate.gif)Attuned to the Dream](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=376930/attuned-to-the-dream): Increases your healing done and the healing you take.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_xavius_dreamsimulacrum.gif)Sleep Walk](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=360806/sleep-walk): Crowd Control (CC) effect. Disorient an enemy, causing them to walk towards you. Damage can break this effect.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_incinerate.gif)Foci of Life](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375574/foci-of-life): Speeds up the healing of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_masterylifebinder_red.gif)Renewing Blaze](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=374348/renewing-blaze)'s HoT.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_flywithme.gif)Rescue](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370665/rescue): Grab an ally within 30 yards, then travel with them up to 30 yards from your initial location. This can be used to move someone up to 60 yards if used at max range. The ally being rescued is able to cast while you taxi them.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_staff_2h_bloodelf_c_01.gif)Lush Growth](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375561/lush-growth): Increases effectiveness of Green spells.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_blue_01.gif)Source of Magic](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369459/source-of-magic): Place a buff on an friendly Healer that returns a small amount of Mana each time you Empower a spell. The mana returned depends on the Empowerment level.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_aerialmastery.gif)Aerial Mastery](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=365933/aerial-mastery): [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) gains an additional charge.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_misc_volatileair.gif)Twin Guardian](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370888/twin-guardian): [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_flywithme.gif)Rescue](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370665/rescue) grants additional movement speed and allows spells to be cast while moving for you and your ally for a short duration.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_magic_managain.gif)Potent Mana](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=418101/potent-mana): [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_blue_01.gif)Source of Magic](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369459/source-of-magic) also increases target's damage and healing.

  - [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_timespiral.gif)Time Spiral](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=374968/time-spiral): Grant allies a free use of their movement ability for a brief time, even if it is on cooldown. For Evokers, this can be used to gain another use of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover). This affects the same spells as [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_blessingofthebronze.gif)Blessing of the Bronze](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=364342/blessing-of-the-bronze).

  - [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_stretchtime.gif)Spatial Paradox](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=406732/spatial-paradox): Allows both a targeted Healer and the Evoker to cast while moving and also increases their range for a short duration. Chooses the nearest Healer within 60 yards if you do not have one targeted.


- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hoverblack.gif)Zephyr](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=374227/zephyr): When activated, this ability increases the movement speed of you and your 4 nearest allies for a short duration. It also reduces the damage that all of you take from AoE attacks.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_quell.gif)Quell](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=351338/quell) ( **Kick**) \- A ranged interrupt on a moderate cooldown.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_ragnaros_lavabolt.gif)Volatility](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369089/volatility) ( **Vol**) \- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) has a chance to bounce and hit an additional enemy.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) enables this ability. **This ability is ranged, despite the animation.**

Effect diminishes with each extension.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_essenceburststacks.gif)Essence Attunement](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375722/essence-attunement) \- You can now hold two [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_essenceburst.gif)Essence Burst](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359618/essence-burst) buffs total.


#### Row 6 Talents

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_inscription_pigment_ruby.gif)Engulfing Blaze](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370837/engulfing-blaze) ( **EnB**) \- Increases both the damage and cast time of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_moltenblood.gif)Heat Wave](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375725/heat-wave) \- Increase the damage of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_blueimmolation.gif)Honed Aggression](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=371038/honed-aggression) \- Increases your chance to Critically Strike.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_arcane_arcanetorrent.gif)Eternity's Span](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375757/eternitys-span) \- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) hits twice as many targets per empowerment level.

- Choice node:

  - [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Eye of Infinity](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=411165/eye-of-infinity) ( **EoI**) \- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) deals increased damage to your primary target.

  - [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_priest_cascade_shadow.gif)Event Horizon](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=411164/event-horizon) ( **EH**) \- Decreases the cooldown of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge).


#### Row 7 Talents

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_masterofelements.gif)Catalyze](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=386283/catalyze) ( **Cata**) \- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath)'s DoT ticks twice as often on targets you channel [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) on.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2.gif)Tyranny](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=376888/tyranny) \- Gain the full effect of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_masterygiantkiller.gif)Mastery: Giantkiller](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362980/mastery-giantkiller) during [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage), regardless of the health of your targets. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) damage also gets the full effect of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_masterygiantkiller.gif)Mastery: Giantkiller](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362980/mastery-giantkiller).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_arcane_arcanepotency.gif)Charged Blast](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370455/charged-blast) ( **CB**) \- Blue damage increases [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) damage with a stacking buff that is consumed with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) is cast. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) s from [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) also consume this effect.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_chargedblast.gif)Shattering Stars](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1265802/shattering-stars) ( **SS**) \- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) now fires off bolts that damage enemies. Damage increased per empowerment level.


#### Row 8 Talents

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/mace_1h_blacksmithing_d_04_icon.gif)Feed the Flames](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369846/feed-the-flames) ( **FtF**) \- After casting [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) a set number of times, your next [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) will explode into a [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firestorm.gif)Firestorm](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=368847/firestorm). This is a ground effect that deals damage over time to enemies that stay inside of it.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Burnout](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375801/burnout) \- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath)'s DoT has a chance to make your next [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) cast instantly. The proc rate is the same, regardless of the number of targets affected by [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_misc_head_dragon_black.gif)Onyx Legacy](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=386348/onyx-legacy) ( **OL**) \- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) cooldown is halved.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_shaman_shockinglava.gif)Spellweaver's Dominance](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370845/spellweavers-dominance) \- Critical Strike damage multiplier (i.e. Causes critical strikes to deal increased damage).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv12_ability_mage_arcanesalvo.gif)Star Salvo](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1265826/star-salvo) \- Increases the damage of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_chargedblast.gif)Shattering Stars](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1265802/shattering-stars), and makes them fire in AoE.


#### Row 9 Talents

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_burningbladeshaman_blazing_radiance.gif)Imminent Destruction](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370781/imminent-destruction) ( **ID**) \- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) increases the damage and lowers the [Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/resource/essence-19) cost of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) for a short duration.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_fontofmagic.gif)Font of Magic](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375783/font-of-magic) \- Empower spells can now be further Empowered by an additional level.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fireresistancetotem_01.gif)Titanic Wrath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=386272/titanic-wrath) ( **TW**) \- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_essenceburst.gif)Essence Burst](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359618/essence-burst) also increases the damage of your next spell that consumes the effect.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_masterylifebinder_blue.gif)Azure Celerity](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1219723/azure-celerity) ( **AC**) \- adds another tick to [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) and slightly increases the cast time.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_powernexus2.gif)Power Swell](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370839/power-swell) ( **PS**) \- Empowering a spell will increase the rate at which your [Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/resource/essence-19) regenerates for a short duration.


#### Row 10 Talents

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_blackattunementalt.gif)Strafing Run](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1266151/strafing-run) ( **SR**) \- Increases [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) damage and allows it to be reactivated for a second cast per use.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_burnout.gif)Scorching Embers](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370819/scorching-embers) ( **SE**) \- Enemies affected by [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) take increased damage from Red spells ( [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre), [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame), [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firestorm.gif)Firestorm](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=368847/firestorm), etc).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_azerite_essence_16.gif)Causality](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375777/causality) \- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) damage reduces the cooldown of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_arcane_arcane03.gif)Scintillation](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370821/scintillation) ( **Scint**) \- Grants [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) a chance on tick to fire a free Level 1 [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) for reduced damage. With [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_arcane_arcanetorrent.gif)Eternity's Span](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375757/eternitys-span), this will cleave an additional target.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_powerswell.gif)Iridescence](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370867/iridescence) ( **Irid**) \- Empowering a spell increases the damage of your next 2 spells of the same color for a short duration.

## Preheat

### Coach, Former RWF Raider, & World of Warcraft Retail Expert

[Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/Preheat)[Discord](https://discord.gg/gd7jEPVcAT)[YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/@Preheat)[Bluesky](https://bsky.app/profile/preheat.bsky.social)[TikTok](https://www.tiktok.com/@preheatwow)[Author Page](https://www.wowhead.com/author/Preheat)

Preheat has raided at the highest level in the game for many years, was a founding member of LIMIT (now [Liquid](https://raider.io/guilds/us/illidan/Liquid)) and was an officer when they got World First. He also participated in the 2nd MDI back in Legion and was Rank 1 that season. He has also commentated multiple community events such as the [Keystone Masters](https://twitter.com/keystonemasters), numerous charity events, and the [Team Liquid](https://twitter.com/TeamLiquid) Race to World First. For the last 3 years, he has helped over 240 players improve their gameplay through [one-on-one coaching](https://metafy.gg/@preheat/sessions).

These days, Preheat focuses on content creation, casting events like the Race to World first, making guides, and coaching. Sharing what he knows and helping others improve is a passion of his and it is a big part of what drives him to keep playing the game.

Outside of World of Warcraft, Preheat has worked in the real world as a software trainer and has almost a decade of experience teaching people, writing documentation, and creating lesson plans for those looking to learn new, technical things.

For all things Evoker and Mage, you can find him in his [Discord](https://discord.gg/HzCKceX), on [Twitter](https://twitter.com/LimitPreheat), posting videos to [YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/c/preheat), and streaming on [Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat).

The **Wowhead Client** is a little application we use to keep our database up to date, and to provide you with some nifty extra functionality on the website!

It serves 2 main purposes:

1. It maintains a WoW addon called the **Wowhead Looter**, which collects data as you play the game!

2. It uploads the **collected data** to Wowhead in order to keep the database up-to-date!


You can also use it to keep track of your completed quests, recipes, mounts, companion pets, and titles!

# Devastation Evoker Rotation Guide - Midnight

[By Preheat](https://www.wowhead.com/author/Preheat)
Updated:
2026/02/25

LivePreheat[Live on Twitch!](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat) Offline on TwitchFollow: Follow [Discord](https://discord.gg/SmWHj8cQp7) [Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat) [Twitter](https://twitter.com/LimitPreheat) [YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/@Preheat)

Welcome to the Devastation Evoker rotation guide for Midnight Season 1! This guide will teach you all about the optimal rotation for Devastation Evoker across its various talent builds. It will cover both core ability cadence and cooldown usage in dungeons and raids for **Midnight Season 1**.

[Overview](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/overview-pve-dps) [BiS Gear](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/bis-gear) [Rotation](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/rotation-cooldowns-pve-dps) [Talent Builds](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/talent-builds-pve-dps) [Consumables](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/enchants-gems-pve-dps) [Stats](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/stat-priority-pve-dps) [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/large/inv_misc_groupneedmore.jpg)\\
Basics](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/basics)

Show Table of Contents

## Devastation Evoker Rotation in Midnight Season 1

Devastation Evoker is a fairly simple and straightforward with its spell priority. Cooldowns are mostly used on cooldown, while building and spending [Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/resource/essence-19) / [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_essenceburst.gif)Essence Burst](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359618/essence-burst).

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| |     |     |
| --- | --- |
| ~~~~ | **Build and Spend:** [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) spend [Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/resource/essence-19), primary resource for Evokers. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_azurestrike.gif)Azure Strike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362969/azure-strike) are the filler spells that generate [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_essenceburst.gif)Essence Burst](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359618/essence-burst), a proc that allows you to use these abilities without spending Essence. Spend Essence and Essence Burst, then use filler spells while waiting. |

* * *

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| ~~~~ | **Use cooldowns frequently:** [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) (Scalecommander) are Devastation's main cooldown. Essence Burst is guaranteed from filler spells during this buff. Its duration can be extended by both [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_nature_shamanrage.gif)Animosity](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375797/animosity). Given that both spells also come with their own short cooldowns, consider what is available when entering Dragonrage and to press them on cooldown during it. |

* * *

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| ~~~~ | **Always be casting:** Try to maintain high uptime on targets. Constant use of damaging spells is the key to high damage in Raid and Mythic+. Use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) effectively to cast spells on the move. Try to fill "downtime" in your rotation with spells, even if its not completely optimal (Azure Strike for instance). Using _something_ is almost always better than being idle. Try to use cooldowns and Empowered spells early and often. | |

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/medium/classicon_evoker_devastation.jpg)Devastation Evoker Starter Guide](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/basics)

## ~~~~ Best Devastation Evoker Rotation

Hero talents also add some flavor to the rotation. Scalecommander uses [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) and Flameshaper monitors [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) duration. Aside from this, they mostly play the same.

Below, we break down the main priority lists for Devastation Evoker: Single-Target Rotation, AoE Rotation, and your opener sequence.

### Priority

01. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) if capped and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) is ready soon. - .

02. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath). \-

03. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) when [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_blackattunementalt.gif)Strafing Run](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1266151/strafing-run) is about to expire. -

04. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage).

05. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales).

06. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) Rank 1.

07. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) Rank 1.

08. Channel [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate). \-

09. Channel [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate). \-

10. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_10_skinning_dragonscales_blue.gif)Azure Sweep](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1265867/azure-sweep).

11. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pupilofalexstraza.gif)Leaping Flames](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369939/leaping-flames) or [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Burnout](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375801/burnout).

12. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) as filler.

13. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_azurestrike.gif)Azure Strike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362969/azure-strike) if needed for movement.


Further Details ~~~~

**Scalecommander** frequently uses [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) even in Single Target. In addition, each time you use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge), you gain a buff that changes [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) into [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate).

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) can be cast while casting, and allows you to cast while moving. With [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_dragonriding_barrelroll01.gif)Slipstream](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441257/slipstream), a [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) charge resets with each [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath). The [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) buff grows in duration with each cast, so be sure to spend a [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) charge before each [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_dragonriding_barrelroll01.gif)Slipstream](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441257/slipstream).

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) is very strong and should be used on cooldown, but you don't want to stay in the animation longer than needed. Use the [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) cancel macro found on the [macros page](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/addons-macro-ui-imports). Here's an example of the macro for [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate):

```
#showtooltip Disintegrate
/cancelaura Deep Breath
/cast Disintegrate
```

This macro will end your [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) with the first press and use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) with the second. You would tap it twice, right as you hit your target.

_The builds used below are covered in the [Talents page](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/talent-builds-pve-dps). Anywhere you see the  icon, you can hover over it for additional notes._

### Priority

1. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage).

2. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales) if [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) is ready.

   - [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales).


3. Cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) to maintain the DoT debuff -

4. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) Rank 1.

5. Channel [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate). \-

6. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_10_skinning_dragonscales_blue.gif)Azure Sweep](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1265867/azure-sweep).

7. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pupilofalexstraza.gif)Leaping Flames](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369939/leaping-flames) or [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Burnout](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375801/burnout).

8. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) as filler.

9. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_azurestrike.gif)Azure Strike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362969/azure-strike) if needed for movement.


Further Details ~~~~

Flameshaper always uses Rank 1 [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath). **Do not use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales) on [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath). This is a massive damage loss.**

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) convert [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) damage over time into explosions via [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_shadowflames_wave.gif)Consume Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=444088/consume-flame).

Wait for [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) to expire, or refresh [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) on targets with <= 30% remaining.

- For dots with pandemic behavior, if the dot has 30% or less of the duration of the refreshing spell, the new duration will be increased without any waste.


### Best Devastation Evoker AoE Rotation

For Devastation, AoE is defined as **3 or more targets**. Devastation plays mostly the same in AoE, aside from sometimes casting [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre). Ranking Empowered spells up can also be beneficial.

With Scalecommander selected, the priority is as follows: With Flameshaper selected, the priority is as follows:

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pupilofalexstraza.gif)Leaping Flames](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369939/leaping-flames) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Burnout](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375801/burnout).
