Further Details ~~~~

In AoE, [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_blackattunementalt.gif)Strafing Run](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1266151/strafing-run) does not have a specific delay. It is simply lower priority than other spells since it is no longer holding up the [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) cooldown.

Currently, it is not worth it to rank Empowers up in Single Target or AoE. Use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) at Rank 1, unless you have [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate).

You do not need to track [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_arcane_arcanepotency.gif)Charged Blast](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370455/charged-blast) stacks, but it is worthwhile to avoid casting [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) in the 5 or so seconds before [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) to gain 3x value on the [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) s.

Swap targets after each [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate) to apply [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_ability_scalecommanderevoker_bombardments.gif)Bombardments](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=434300/bombardments) to multiple targets to get more value on [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_divideandconquer.gif)Extended Battle](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441212/extended-battle).

_The builds used below are covered in the [Talents page](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/talent-builds-pve-dps). Anywhere you see the  icon, you can hover over it for additional notes._

### ~~~~ Options

![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/medium/mace_1h_blacksmithing_d_04_icon.jpg) Feed the Flame Build

### Priority

01. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage).

02. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales) on [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge). \- .

03. Cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) Rank 1 to maintain the DoT debuff -

04. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) Rank 1.

05. Cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre).

06. Cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) at **4+ targets**.

07. Otherwise, channel [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate). \-

08. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_10_skinning_dragonscales_blue.gif)Azure Sweep](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1265867/azure-sweep).

09. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pupilofalexstraza.gif)Leaping Flames](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369939/leaping-flames) or [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Burnout](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375801/burnout).

10. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_azurestrike.gif)Azure Strike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362969/azure-strike) as filler.


Further Details ~~~~

Rank 1 [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) before [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage). The 3x [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) s convert [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) damage over time into explosions via [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_shadowflames_wave.gif)Consume Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=444088/consume-flame).

### Best Devastation Evoker Opener

The optimal Devastation Evoker opener sets up your [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) and uses various cooldowns, trinkets, or items. Following that, we transition into our main rotation.

### Opener Sequence

01. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) twice before combat. - .

02. Pre-cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame).

03. Use damage potion

04. AoE Only: Use [![Vaelgor's Final Stare](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_trinket_raid_voidspire_int1_voiddragoneye.gif)Vaelgor's Final Stare](https://www.wowhead.com/item=249346/vaelgors-final-stare).

05. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath). \-

06. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage).

07. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales).

08. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge).

09. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) Rank 1.

10. AoE Only: [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) again.

11. Cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_10_skinning_dragonscales_blue.gif)Azure Sweep](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1265867/azure-sweep) twice. -

12. Channel [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate). \-

13. _[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) is now active, continue to the normal rotation._


Outside of AoE: Delay your damage trinket (ex: [![Vaelgor's Final Stare](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_trinket_raid_voidspire_int1_voiddragoneye.gif)Vaelgor's Final Stare](https://www.wowhead.com/item=249346/vaelgors-final-stare)) until you have 4 stacks of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv12_apextalent_evoker_risingfury.gif)Rising Fury](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1271687/rising-fury).

1. Pre-cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame).

2. Use damage potion

3. AoE Only: [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) Rank 1.

4. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage).

5. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales).

6. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge).

7. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) Rank 1.

8. Channel [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate), or cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) for AoE.

9. _[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) is now active, continue to the normal rotation._


Delay your damage trinket (ex: [![Vaelgor's Final Stare](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_trinket_raid_voidspire_int1_voiddragoneye.gif)Vaelgor's Final Stare](https://www.wowhead.com/item=249346/vaelgors-final-stare)) until you have 4 stacks of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv12_apextalent_evoker_risingfury.gif)Rising Fury](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1271687/rising-fury).

### Pre-Combat Check

Before any combat happens, it is important for Devastation Evoker to make sure the following are done. This applies to raids, dungeons, delves, or even open-world content.

- **Cast Group Buffs -** Use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_blessingofthebronze.gif)Blessing of the Bronze](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=364342/blessing-of-the-bronze) to buff you and your allies.

- **Heal up -** Use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) / [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_emeraldblossom.gif)Emerald Blossom](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=365261/emerald-blossom) / [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_rescue.gif)Verdant Embrace](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=360995/verdant-embrace) to heal yourself and allies. This will fill your [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_bijou_red.gif)Scarlet Adaptation](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=372469/scarlet-adaptation) buff, if it does not overheal.

- **Consider Threat (Optional) -** Think twice about going full blast if Tanks are inexperienced. You have no way to drop aggro if you pull before Tanks establish threat.


## ~~~~ Devastation Evoker Major Cooldowns

Devastation Evoker has one main cooldown: [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage). Other cooldowns are minor, or group buffs. The below section covers _how_ they work, and most importantly, how to get the most out of each use.

Dragonrage

Empower Spells

Tip The Scales

Deep Breath

Fury of the Aspects

### Dragonrage

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) is your main cooldown as Devastation. This buff guarantees [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_essenceburst.gif)Essence Burst](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359618/essence-burst) procs from [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_azurestrike.gif)Azure Strike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362969/azure-strike) for the duration. Try to use it every 2 minutes.

Other key takeaways are:

- Activating this spell fires 3 [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) s at random targets.

- Gain the full effect of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_masterygiantkiller.gif)Mastery: Giantkiller](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362980/mastery-giantkiller) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2.gif)Tyranny](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=376888/tyranny).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) extend the buff with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_nature_shamanrage.gif)Animosity](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375797/animosity).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) can be held for AoE, but it is a fairly long buff. The average duration should be around 32 seconds with proper play.

- Apex talents buff your damage during this cooldown ( [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv12_apextalent_evoker_risingfury.gif)Rising Fury](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1271687/rising-fury) / [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv12_apextalent_evoker_risingfury.gif)Rising Fury](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1271796/rising-fury))

  - Gain a damage buff after [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) expires with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv12_apextalent_evoker_risingfury.gif)Rising Fury](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1271788/rising-fury).


### Empower Spells

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) are Devastation's Empower spells. These can be ranked up for different effects.

Empowering [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) moves damage from the debuff it applies into the initial hit of upfront damage. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pupilofalexstraza.gif)Leaping Flames](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=369939/leaping-flames) also increases in target count per Empower level. The DoT component from [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) scales dynamically with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_masterygiantkiller.gif)Mastery: Giantkiller](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362980/mastery-giantkiller), which means that the damage goes down as the target loses health.

Empowering [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) increases the target count, and has an affect on damage talents like [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_chargedblast.gif)Shattering Stars](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1265802/shattering-stars). [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_arcane_arcanetorrent.gif)Eternity's Span](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375757/eternitys-span) doubles the target count per level.

* * *

For Flameshaper, [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) (without [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales)) should generally be used at rank 1 in Single Target and AoE.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) has two charges.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_shadowflames_wave.gif)Consume Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=444088/consume-flame) uses [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) to fuel explosions. This chips away at remaining duration.


For Scalecommander, [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) (without [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales)) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) should generally be used at rank 1 in Single Target and AoE.

- Each use of Empowered spells will enable [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate) marks the target for [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_ability_scalecommanderevoker_bombardments.gif)Bombardments](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=434300/bombardments). This enables a damage proc for you and allies.


### Tip The Scales

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales) is a minor cooldown. It is used in [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) to instantly cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) or [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) at max rank.

* * *

For Flameshaper, [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales) should only be used on [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge). Higher [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) uptime is better for [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_burnout.gif)Scorching Embers](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370819/scorching-embers), [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Burnout](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375801/burnout) procs, [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) damage via [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_masterofelements.gif)Catalyze](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=386283/catalyze), and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_shadowflames_wave.gif)Consume Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=444088/consume-flame).

For Scalecommander, [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales) can be used on either [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) or [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath).

### Deep Breath

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) is another notable cooldown. This spell always gets full [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_masterygiantkiller.gif)Mastery: Giantkiller](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362980/mastery-giantkiller) value with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2.gif)Tyranny](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=376888/tyranny). Optimal usage depends on your Hero Talents, but it can also be used for movement or to avoid/break crowd control (stuns and roots). Always use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) at minimum range unless you need it for movement. **It is important to remember that while flying, effects on the ground will still hit you, and you will be locked into the animation.** Some class talents modify this ability:

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_burningbladeshaman_blazing_radiance.gif)Imminent Destruction](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370781/imminent-destruction) gives you a short buff that decreases [Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/resource/essence-19) costs and increases damage.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_blackattunementalt.gif)Strafing Run](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1266151/strafing-run) increases damage and allows for a second use.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_misc_head_dragon_black.gif)Onyx Legacy](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=386348/onyx-legacy) reduces the cooldown.

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_timedilation.gif)Stretch Time](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=410352/stretch-time) takes any damage taken during the skill and stretches it out, adding some safety.


* * *

Scalecommander uses this spell on cooldown, due to talents like [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Maneuverability](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=433871/maneuverability) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_dragonriding_dynamicflight01.gif)Command Squadron](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1260745/command-squadron). This version of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) can be steered, and you can eject from it.

- Cooldown is further reduced by [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_ability_scalecommanderevoker_bombardments.gif)Bombardments](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=434300/bombardments) procs via [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_soar.gif)Wingleader](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441206/wingleader).

- Your Z-axis is preserved during this spell, so long as you don't fly uphill. Use height to your advantage if you plan to fly over rocky or dangerous terrain.


Flameshaper does not typically use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) for damage, unless talents like [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_burningbladeshaman_blazing_radiance.gif)Imminent Destruction](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370781/imminent-destruction) are taken.

### Fury of the Aspects

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_furyoftheaspects.gif)Fury of the Aspects](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=390386/fury-of-the-aspects) is a group cooldown, which means that when to use it is often a group decision. This spell grants you and your allies the [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_nature_bloodlust.gif)Bloodlust](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=2825/bloodlust) effect, increasing your Haste. This spell is off the global cooldown.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_furyoftheaspects.gif)Fury of the Aspects](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=390386/fury-of-the-aspects) applies the [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_nature_sleep.gif)Exhaustion](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=57723/exhaustion) debuff, locking players out of receiving similar effects for 10 minutes.

## ~~~~ Devastation Evoker Advanced Tips

The best thing you can do to improve your damage as a Devastation Evoker is to always be casting. Idle time in combat, caused by inactivity or poor movement, adds up and will greatly affect your damage. Mastering movement with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) and keeping focus during combat is unequivocally the best way to raise your damage.

Hover and Movement

Deep Breath Z-axis

Disintegrate Chaining and Clipping

### Hover and Movement

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) is your most important tool for minimizing damage losses due to movement. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) can be cast while casting, and allows you to cast on the move. Subsequent [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) casts add to the duration, meaning you can use it again while the buff is up without wasting duration. Any time you cancel a cast because you needed to move, it created a small period of downtime. These add up and greatly reduce your damage.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_glide.gif)Glide](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358733/glide) is also very useful for movement, since using it is much faster than running. Simply tap your spacebar for a boost. Keep in mind, this will not work during normal casts. The trick with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_glide.gif)Glide](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358733/glide) is to triple tap, since canceling it removes the slowfall effect. This makes you travel faster, and hit the ground quicker. Bonus tip: Some ground effects can be avoided if you are at the height of your [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_glide.gif)Glide](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358733/glide).

Another interesting quirk of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) is that it allows you to [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_glide.gif)Glide](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358733/glide) while channeling [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate). This is very powerful, since it allows you to quickly reposition by tapping your spacebar. Combine [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_glide.gif)Glide](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358733/glide) canceling to quickly hop around the battlefield while channeling.

Not all downtime is created equal. During [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage), stakes are higher. As Scalecommander, getting full value from [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate) is also very important. For these reasons, I recommend always having a [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) in hand for cooldowns. Scalecommander has a much easier time with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover), since each [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) refunds a charge via [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_dragonriding_barrelroll01.gif)Slipstream](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441257/slipstream). Make sure you make room for this ahead of time, or the value is wasted.

Aside from [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath), all spells can be cast on the move with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover).

### Deep Breath Z-axis

As Scalecommander, [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) works a bit differently due to [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Maneuverability](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=433871/maneuverability). One interesting property is that it preserves your Z-axis while you are flying. This can be good or bad, depending on the situation. A common situation you want to avoid is using [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) uphill. Once you start scraping the ground, even a pebble can stop you in your tracks. Instead, try to gain the high ground before using [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath).

Another trick is to [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_glide.gif)Glide](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358733/glide) to gain altitude before [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath). This helps greatly on level surfaces, since even a little added height often allows you to fly over obstacles that would normally stop you in your tracks. In some cases, added height also allows you to avoid damage ground mechanics.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) can also be used in tandem to cross great distances in the air. You can try this yourself: Start by jump [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_glide.gif)Glide](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358733/glide) ing from a high place. Use both [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) charges to dash forward twice, then use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) until it expires. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) again, then [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) forward twice using the refunds from [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_dragonriding_barrelroll01.gif)Slipstream](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441257/slipstream). [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_timespiral.gif)Time Spiral](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=374968/time-spiral) for one last forward [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover), then finish by reactivating [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_racial_glide.gif)Glide](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358733/glide). In total, this is around 200 yards of movement with minimal loss of height.

### Disintegrate Chaining and Clipping

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) has 5 ticks with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_masterylifebinder_blue.gif)Azure Celerity](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1219723/azure-celerity) and 4 ticks without. If channeling [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate), you can cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) again after the 4th tick without losing damage. This causes the new [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) to chain in a similar "Pandemic" fashion and moves remaining duration and final tick of the previous channel to the new one. This is chaining, and you don't lose any value doing this. As a general rule, you always want to avoid downtime by chaining [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) after the penultimate tick.

As Scalecommander, avoid chaining a [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate) into a normal [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) as this will cause you to lose a percentage of the buff equal to how many ticks you clipped.

Clipping is when you cut [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) short by either interrupting it with another spell or Chaining too early. Clipping is best avoided, but can be a small gain under certain circumstances. Currently, clipping non- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate) [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) one tick early, by interrupting it for Empowers or [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath), is a very small ( **0.4%**) gain for Scalecommander.

If you mess clipping up the loss will outweigh the gain, so decide for yourself if the juice is worth the squeeze.

### Chaining Example

Example [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) channel (imagine this is your castbar):

|     |     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 5 \|-------------------------\| 4 | \|-------------------------\| 3 | \|-------------------------\| 2 | \|-------------------------\| 1 |

Channels go from right to left. The first tick happens when the [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) is cast. The last tick is when the channel ends.

Between 4 and 5 is ok to chain

* * *

Let's assume you chain a second [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) on the red line.

|     |     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 5 \|-------------\|-----------\| 4 | \|------------------------\| 3 | \|------------------------\| 2 | \|------------------------\| 1 |

The remainder on this last tick gets carried into the new channel. This means the new [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) has 6 ticks.

|     |     |     |     |     |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 6 \|-----------------------\| 5 | \|------------------------\| 4 | \|------------------------\| 3 | \|------------------------\| 2 | \|-------------\| 1 |

Our Devastation Evoker guides are always updated with the latest information from in-game experience, simulations, and logs. Make sure to check our changelog for this page by clicking on **View Changelog** in the Quick Facts column or on the book icon at the top of the page. If you are interested in more in-depth Devastation Evoker guides for **Midnight Season 1**, make sure to browse the _Navigation Bar_ at the top of the page, along with our list of **Related Guides** just beneath the _Table of Contents_.Bank 1 [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_fireball.gif)Fire Blast](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=108853/fire-blast) for [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_burningsoul.gif)Glorious Incandescence](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=449394/glorious-incandescence), but make sure to use them all before [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_sealoffire.gif)Combustion](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=190319/combustion) ends.If you are above 104% haste, don't attempt to get [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_ironmaidens_grapeshotblast.gif)Hot Streak!](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=48108/hot-streak).

Cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_flamebolt.gif)Fireball](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=133/fireball) instead of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Scorch](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=2948/scorch) if you already have a [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_ironmaidens_grapeshotblast.gif)Hot Streak!](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=48108/hot-streak).

This is due to extreme Haste breaking Double Pyroblast. The haste value where [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Scorch](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=2948/scorch) stop working can be lower depending on your connection to the server.

If you are already casting [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_flamebolt.gif)Fireball](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=133/fireball)/ [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_ability_frostfiremage_frostfirebolt.gif)Frostfire Bolt](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=431044/frostfire-bolt) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_sealoffire.gif)Combustion](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=190319/combustion) is suddenly ready you can use that cast to enter [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_sealoffire.gif)Combustion](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=190319/combustion). [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Scorch](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=2948/scorch) can also be used on the move.

Prioritize spending all [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_fireball.gif)Fire Blast](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=108853/fire-blast) charges in [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_sealoffire.gif)Combustion](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=190319/combustion) over spending all [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_shaman_lavasurge.gif)Pyroclasm](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=269650/pyroclasm).

Stop using [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_fireball.gif)Fire Blast](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=108853/fire-blast) charges and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_shaman_lavasurge.gif)Pyroclasm](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=269650/pyroclasm) buffs if [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_sealoffire.gif)Combustion](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=190319/combustion) will be up soon to ensure they are available during cooldowns.

If [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_sealoffire.gif)Combustion](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=190319/combustion) is ready, hard-cast a spell and activate [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_sealoffire.gif)Combustion](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=190319/combustion) right before the cast completes. Priority of spells to hard-cast:

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_fireball02.gif)Pyroblast](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=11366/pyroblast) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_shaman_lavasurge.gif)Pyroclasm](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=269650/pyroclasm).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_flamebolt.gif)Fireball](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=133/fireball).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Scorch](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=2948/scorch) on the move.


If [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_sealoffire.gif)Combustion](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=190319/combustion) is ready, cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_mage_meteor.gif)Meteor](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=153561/meteor) and activate [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_sealoffire.gif)Combustion](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=190319/combustion). If [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_mage_meteor.gif)Meteor](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=153561/meteor) isn't avialable, hard-cast a spell and activate [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_sealoffire.gif)Combustion](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=190319/combustion) right before the cast completes. Priority of spells to hard-cast:

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_fireball02.gif)Pyroblast](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=11366/pyroblast) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_shaman_lavasurge.gif)Pyroclasm](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=269650/pyroclasm).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_ability_frostfiremage_frostfirebolt.gif)Frostfire Bolt](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=431044/frostfire-bolt) without [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_frostfire-orb.gif)Frostfire Empowerment](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=431176/frostfire-empowerment).

- [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_soulburn.gif)Scorch](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=2948/scorch) on the move.


Two simultaneous [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_selfdestruct.gif)Flamestrike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=2120/flamestrike) casts (one hard-cast, one instant with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_mage_hotstreak.gif)Hot Streak](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=195283/hot-streak)) will "munch" the proc and not result in a [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_mage_hotstreak.gif)Hot Streak](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=195283/hot-streak).

Hard-cast [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_selfdestruct.gif)Flamestrike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=2120/flamestrike) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_mage_hotstreak.gif)Heating Up](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=48107/heating-up) _only_ then consume the generated [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_mage_hotstreak.gif)Hot Streak](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=195283/hot-streak) after.

Avoid hard-casting [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_selfdestruct.gif)Flamestrike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=2120/flamestrike) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_shaman_lavasurge.gif)Pyroclasm](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=269650/pyroclasm) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_mage_hotstreak.gif)Hot Streak](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=195283/hot-streak) already active.

The goal here is to proc [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_shaman_lavasurge.gif)Pyroclasm](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=269650/pyroclasm) before the pull without entering combat.

You must activate [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_sealoffire.gif)Combustion](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=190319/combustion) in the last 150 milliseconds of the [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_fireball02.gif)Pyroblast](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=11366/pyroblast) cast for this opener.

Distance to the target is also a concern, avoid long travel times.

Only use this opener only if you are confident in your timing.[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_flamebolt.gif)Fireball](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=133/fireball) and the instant [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_fireball02.gif)Pyroblast](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=11366/pyroblast) leave your hands at the same time. Both crit. This means you get [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_mage_hotstreak.gif)Hot Streak](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=195283/hot-streak) for another [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_fireball02.gif)Pyroblast](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=11366/pyroblast). The purpose here to proc [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_essenceburststacks.gif)Essence Bomb](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1236792/essence-bomb) via [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_essenceburst.gif)Essence Burst](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359618/essence-burst) as quickly as possible.Generally we want to avoid using [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_azurestrike.gif)Azure Strike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362969/azure-strike) as Flameshaper, but with extreme haste this can get us another Essence Bomb. This GCD is also a good time to [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) if needed. [(Spell #443328)](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=443328) damage on the target is greatly increased by the damage over time (DoT) effects. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_tradeskillitem_lessersorcerersfire.gif)Ruby Embers](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=365937/ruby-embers), [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_10_elementalcombinedfoozles_purifiedshadowflame.gif)Enkindle](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=444016/enkindle), & [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) must be present when you cast it for optimal gameplay.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_tradeskillitem_lessersorcerersfire.gif)Ruby Embers](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=365937/ruby-embers) \- This DoT comes from [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame). You only need 1 stack on the target.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_10_elementalcombinedfoozles_purifiedshadowflame.gif)Enkindle](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=444016/enkindle) \- This DoT comes from [Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/resource/essence-19) spenders. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) casts 3 [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) s which also apply it.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) \- With [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_fire_burnout.gif)Scorching Embers](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370819/scorching-embers), max rank [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) so long as you can [(Spell #443328)](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=443328) while it is active. If [(Spell #443328)](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=443328) is not ready, use at rank 1. Higher ranks do more damage but make the window smaller.

[(Spell #370452)](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370452) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_powerswell.gif)Iridescence](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370867/iridescence) also further increase the damage of [(Spell #443328)](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=443328).

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_arcane_arcanepotency.gif)Charged Blast](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370455/charged-blast) stacks to 20 and increases damage of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre). The exact value of stacks that makes Pyre worth casting depends.

12 stacks is the recommendation at 3 targets.

Scalecommander gains about 0.3% damage waiting for 16 stacks at 3 targets. The difference in minimal, so 12 is the overall recommendation for the sake of simplicity.

If stacks are available for [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage), they increase the damage of all 3 Pyres it casts. You must have enough Haste to still get 4 Empowered spells cast during [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) for your extensions (via [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_nature_shamanrage.gif)Animosity](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375797/animosity)) for this to be worth it. If your [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) is less than 38 seconds on the pull, then save [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) to cast during [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage).Mastery trinkets like [![House of Cards](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_111_gallyjack_gallywix.gif)House of Cards](https://www.wowhead.com/item=230027/house-of-cards) (and new Season 3 ones) are the highest priority here, followed by others effects like [![Best-in-Slots](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_mace_2h_goblinraid_d_02.gif)Best-in-Slots](https://www.wowhead.com/item=232526/best-in-slots). Other trinkets or [![Neural Synapse Enhancer](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_staff_2h_mechagnome_c_01.gif)Neural Synapse Enhancer](https://www.wowhead.com/item=168973/neural-synapse-enhancer) can also be used here if you have them.[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales) is off the Global Cooldown and can be cast ahead of time. Empowering this spell higher adds more targets.

Rank 1: 1-2 Targets

Rank 2: 3-4 Targets

Rank 3: 5-6 Targets

Rank 4: 7-8 Targets ( [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_fontofmagic.gif)Font of Magic](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375783/font-of-magic))

Empowering this spell increases damage, but shortens the damage over time.

Rank 1: During [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage).

Rank 2: Outside of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage).

Rank is flexible, and can be increased in AoE or if targets will die soon.

With [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_dragonriding_barrelroll01.gif)Slipstream](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=441257/slipstream), [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) resets the cooldown on [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover), avoid wasting charges by overcapping.

Hover can be used in place, and can be cast while casting.

If you are channeling [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate) and do not have another buff, fully channel [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) until the end.

If you have two stacks of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate), you can chain these casts.

Chaining means casting another [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) before the current channel ends, between the penultimate tick and the end of the channel. This transfers remaining bit of progress towards the last tick to the new channel without losing a tick.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate) will hit any valid target (in range) in front of your character. Turning your character towards additional targets in cleave will help you get more [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage2_blue.gif)Mass Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=436335/mass-disintegrate) hits.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) channels can be chained.

Chaining means casting another [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) before the current channel ends, between the penultimate tick and the end of the channel. This transfers remaining bit of progress towards the last tick to the new channel without losing a tick.

Clipping means losing a tick. This can mean you interrupted your [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) channel with another spell, or chained earlier than the penultimate tick. Clipping is not recommended in most cases, but we will expand on it in the Advanced section.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) Damage over time (DoT) should always be applied to enemies, but the duration will quickly disappear with using [Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/resource/essence-19) spenders via [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_shadowflames_wave.gif)Consume Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=444088/consume-flame).

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) can be reapplied to targets once the DoT is refreshable, meaning it has <= 30% duration remaining.

Always use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) Rank 1.

Requires Season 1 Midnight tier set ( [Livery of the Black Talon](https://www.wowhead.com/item-set=1981/livery-of-the-black-talon) ).

- Azure Sweep damage increased by 50% and its damage against your primary target is increased by an additional 100%.

* * *

- Eternity Surge grants 1 additional charge of Azure Sweep. Casting Azure Sweep reduces the cooldown of Eternity Surge by 2.0 sec.


[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) can be used to "overconsume" the [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) duration via [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_shadowflames_wave.gif)Consume Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=444088/consume-flame).

When [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) is cast, it does not check the current [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) duration for [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_shadowflames_wave.gif)Consume Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=444088/consume-flame) to deal damage. It only checks if the debuff is present.

Since [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) uses 3 [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) that all hit at the same time, it is worthwhile to shorten [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) ahead of this to make use of the overconsumption.

In Single Target, try to spend as little time in [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) as possible. Cancel [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) as soon as you make contact.

Delay your second [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) until [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_blackattunementalt.gif)Strafing Run](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=1266151/strafing-run) is about to expire. Spacing these out in Single Target is worthwhile. In AOE, you can send them back to back.

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) are roughly equal in terms of [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales) value, up 4 targets.

At 5+ targets, [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) pulls ahead as the preferred option.

If [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) is not ready when [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_tipthescales.gif)Tip the Scales](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=370553/tip-the-scales) is up, just send it on [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath).

Notes will appear here

![](https://wow.zamimg.com/uploads/author-pages/author/733b7c6f63a7c37c3e137cfd4295d454458eb812.jpg)

## Preheat

### Coach, Former RWF Raider, & World of Warcraft Retail Expert

[Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/Preheat)[Discord](https://discord.gg/gd7jEPVcAT)[YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/@Preheat)[Bluesky](https://bsky.app/profile/preheat.bsky.social)[TikTok](https://www.tiktok.com/@preheatwow)[Author Page](https://www.wowhead.com/author/Preheat)

Preheat has raided at the highest level in the game for many years, was a founding member of LIMIT (now [Liquid](https://raider.io/guilds/us/illidan/Liquid)) and was an officer when they got World First. He also participated in the 2nd MDI back in Legion and was Rank 1 that season. He has also commentated multiple community events such as the [Keystone Masters](https://twitter.com/keystonemasters), numerous charity events, and the [Team Liquid](https://twitter.com/TeamLiquid) Race to World First. For the last 3 years, he has helped over 240 players improve their gameplay through [one-on-one coaching](https://metafy.gg/@preheat/sessions).

These days, Preheat focuses on content creation, casting events like the Race to World first, making guides, and coaching. Sharing what he knows and helping others improve is a passion of his and it is a big part of what drives him to keep playing the game.

Outside of World of Warcraft, Preheat has worked in the real world as a software trainer and has almost a decade of experience teaching people, writing documentation, and creating lesson plans for those looking to learn new, technical things.

For all things Evoker and Mage, you can find him in his [Discord](https://discord.gg/HzCKceX), on [Twitter](https://twitter.com/LimitPreheat), posting videos to [YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/c/preheat), and streaming on [Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat).

None of the guides online seem to know how to play Flameshaper. Engulf damage is increased by the number of DOTs you have on the target.

You should have the Living Flame dot talent (Ruby Embers, which stacks x3 but unclear if each stack counts separately for Engulf), Flame Breath, and the Disintegrate dot talent (Enkindle) active on the target prior to using Engulf. I also use the extended Shattering Star time with Focusing Iris, but that's personal preference.

My opener is Living Flame, Dragonrage, Flame Breath, Shattering Star, Essence Burst, Disintegrate, up to two instant proc Living Flames, then Engulf x2.

I'm consistently one of the top DPS in my guild raids; I come to these guides to help improve even more but I'm met with nonsense like what's written here; at least explain the rotation you're using. The top 50 Evokers on WarcraftLogs are Scalecommander which sucks to play, so there's basically no reliable guidance for Flameshaper.

Last edited by [Madisonics](https://www.wowhead.com/user=Madisonics) on 2024/10/22 (Patch 11.0.2)

The **Wowhead Client** is a little application we use to keep our database up to date, and to provide you with some nifty extra functionality on the website!

It serves 2 main purposes:

1. It maintains a WoW addon called the **Wowhead Looter**, which collects data as you play the game!

2. It uploads the **collected data** to Wowhead in order to keep the database up-to-date!


You can also use it to keep track of your completed quests, recipes, mounts, companion pets, and titles!

So, what are you waiting for? [Download the client](https://www.wowhead.com/client) and get started.

# Devastation Evoker Enchants & Consumables - Midnight

[By Preheat](https://www.wowhead.com/author/Preheat)
Updated:
2026/03/01

LivePreheat[Live on Twitch!](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat) Offline on TwitchFollow: Follow [Discord](https://discord.gg/SmWHj8cQp7) [Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat) [Twitter](https://twitter.com/LimitPreheat) [YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/@Preheat)

Consumables are an important aspect of high-level content in WoW, such as raids and Mythic+ dungeons. They provide additional ways for players to improve and customize their stats outside of gear.

In this guide, we will explain the best gems, flasks, potions, and enchants to use for Devastation Evoker in **Midnight Season 1**, as well as cheaper alternatives. Make sure to also check our [Midnight Profession Guide](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/professions/overview) for all profession details.

[Overview](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/overview-pve-dps) [BiS Gear](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/bis-gear) [Rotation](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/rotation-cooldowns-pve-dps) [Talent Builds](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/talent-builds-pve-dps) [Consumables](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/enchants-gems-pve-dps) [Stats](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/stat-priority-pve-dps) [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/large/inv_misc_groupneedmore.jpg)\\
Basics](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/basics)

| Gems | One [![Indecipherable Eversong Diamond](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_jewelcrafting_epic_gem_cut_green.gif)Indecipherable Eversong Diamond![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=240983/indecipherable-eversong-diamond) with a mix of [![Flawless Quick Garnet](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_jewelcrafting_rare_gem_cut_nature_red.gif)Flawless Quick Garnet![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=240906/flawless-quick-garnet) and [![Flawless Masterful Garnet](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_jewelcrafting_rare_gem_cut_void_red.gif)Flawless Masterful Garnet![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=240908/flawless-masterful-garnet). |

### Devastation Evoker Gems

For maximum damage, you will want to use [![Indecipherable Eversong Diamond](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_jewelcrafting_epic_gem_cut_green.gif)Indecipherable Eversong Diamond![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=240983/indecipherable-eversong-diamond) as your Diamond, and a mix of [![Flawless Quick Garnet](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_jewelcrafting_rare_gem_cut_nature_red.gif)Flawless Quick Garnet![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=240906/flawless-quick-garnet) and [![Flawless Masterful Garnet](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_jewelcrafting_rare_gem_cut_void_red.gif)Flawless Masterful Garnet![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=240908/flawless-masterful-garnet) in all other sockets.

[![Powerful Eversong Diamond](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_jewelcrafting_epic_gem_cut_orange.gif)Powerful Eversong Diamond![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=240967/powerful-eversong-diamond) is also very close, but requires a combination of at least 1 Peridot, 1 Lapis, 1 Garnet, and 1 Amethyst. [![Indecipherable Eversong Diamond](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_jewelcrafting_epic_gem_cut_green.gif)Indecipherable Eversong Diamond![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=240983/indecipherable-eversong-diamond) does not require as many gem slots. As always, sim yourself.

- **Peridot**: [![Flawless Deadly Peridot](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_jewelcrafting_rare_gem_cut_fire_green.gif)Flawless Deadly Peridot![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=240890/flawless-deadly-peridot)

- **Amethyst**: [![Flawless Deadly Amethyst](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_jewelcrafting_rare_gem_cut_fire_purple.gif)Flawless Deadly Amethyst![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=240898/flawless-deadly-amethyst)

- **Garnet**: [![Flawless Quick Garnet](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_jewelcrafting_rare_gem_cut_nature_red.gif)Flawless Quick Garnet![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=240906/flawless-quick-garnet)

- **Lapis**: [![Flawless Deadly Lapis](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_jewelcrafting_rare_gem_cut_fire_blue.gif)Flawless Deadly Lapis![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=240914/flawless-deadly-lapis)


### Devastation Evoker Weapon Enchant

Early on, [![Enchant Weapon - Acuity of the Ren'dorei](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_enchanting_enchantedvellum_purple.gif)Enchant Weapon - Acuity of the Ren'dorei![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=244029/enchant-weapon-acuity-of-the-rendorei) is going to be your weapon enchant. As the expansion goes on, secondary stat-based weapon enchants will likely pull ahead. Use [Raidbots](https://www.raidbots.com/simbot) to sim your own character.

## ~~~~ Best Devastation Evoker Consumables in Midnight Season 1

Below, we've listed the best flasks, potions, and weapon buffs that Devastation Evokers can use in PvE content. While Haste is generally recommended as the safest option for gearing characters and for the added benefit of smoothing out gameplay feel, you should ideally be simming your character using [raidbots](https://www.raidbots.com/simbot) to determine the best configuration for you.

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| **Type** | **Best** |
| Flask | [![Flask of the Shattered Sun](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_alchemy_flask_sindoreipotion_red--.gif)Flask of the Shattered Sun![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=241326/flask-of-the-shattered-sun)<br>[![Flask of the Magisters](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_alchemy_flask_sindoreipotion_black.gif)Flask of the Magisters![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=241322/flask-of-the-magisters) |
| Combat Potion | [![Potion of Recklessness](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_alchemy_voidpotion_red.gif)Potion of Recklessness![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=241288/potion-of-recklessness) |
| Health Potion | [![Silvermoon Health Potion](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_alchemy_lightpotion_orange.gif)Silvermoon Health Potion![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=241304/silvermoon-health-potion) |
| Weapon Buff | [![Thalassian Phoenix Oil](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_enchanting_manaoil_red.gif)Thalassian Phoenix Oil![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=243734/thalassian-phoenix-oil) |
| Augment Rune | [![Void-Touched Augment Rune](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_10_enchanting_crystal_color2.gif)Void-Touched Augment Rune](https://www.wowhead.com/item=259085/void-touched-augment-rune) |
| Food | [![Harandar Celebration](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_misc_1h_soup_b_01_misc_1h_soup_b_01.gif)Harandar Celebration](https://www.wowhead.com/item=255846/harandar-celebration)<br>[![Quel'dorei Medley](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_cooking_10_draconicdelicacies.gif)Quel'dorei Medley](https://www.wowhead.com/item=242272/queldorei-medley) |
| Tea | [![Sanguithorn Tea](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_cooking_tea_purple.gif)Sanguithorn Tea](https://www.wowhead.com/item=242299/sanguithorn-tea) |

Tea is a new cooking consumable that stacks with your food buff. These offer bonuses to professions, but also grant 50 speed.

### Devastation Evoker Flask

The best Flask for Devastation Evokers is [![Flask of the Shattered Sun](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_alchemy_flask_sindoreipotion_red--.gif)Flask of the Shattered Sun![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=241326/flask-of-the-shattered-sun). [![Flask of the Magisters](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_alchemy_flask_sindoreipotion_black.gif)Flask of the Magisters![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=241322/flask-of-the-magisters) and [![Flask of the Blood Knights](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_alchemy_flask_sindoreipotion_white-.gif)Flask of the Blood Knights![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=241324/flask-of-the-blood-knights) can also be good to push your Haste and Mastery above Versatility for [![Potion of Recklessness](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_alchemy_voidpotion_red.gif)Potion of Recklessness![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=241288/potion-of-recklessness).

### Devastation Evoker Combat Potions

When it comes to combat potions, they are both close. [![Potion of Recklessness](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_alchemy_voidpotion_red.gif)Potion of Recklessness![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=241288/potion-of-recklessness) is best if your stats are good. [![Light's Potential](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_alchemy_lightpotion_yellow.gif)Light's Potential![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=241308/lights-potential) is slightly worse, but does not have any special requirements.

### Devastation Evoker Health Potions

The best healing potion for Devastation Evokers is [![Silvermoon Health Potion](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_alchemy_lightpotion_orange.gif)Silvermoon Health Potion![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=241304/silvermoon-health-potion).

### Devastation Evoker Weapon Buff

For their weapons, Devastation Evokers will always use [![Thalassian Phoenix Oil](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_enchanting_manaoil_red.gif)Thalassian Phoenix Oil![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/TextureAtlas/live/professions-chaticon-quality-tier2.png)](https://www.wowhead.com/item=243734/thalassian-phoenix-oil).

### Devastation Evoker Augment Rune

In Midnight, the new Augment Rune is [![Void-Touched Augment Rune](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_10_enchanting_crystal_color2.gif)Void-Touched Augment Rune](https://www.wowhead.com/item=259085/void-touched-augment-rune). These provide a small gain, but are likely to be expensive. Augment runes are not necessary for most players!

### Devastation Evoker Food

In Midnight, personal stat food is no better than feast buffs. Intellect food is equivalent to Secondary stats. As always, sim your character to check which food is the best for your configuration of Secondary stats.

- Feast: [![Harandar Celebration](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_misc_1h_soup_b_01_misc_1h_soup_b_01.gif)Harandar Celebration](https://www.wowhead.com/item=255846/harandar-celebration) and [![Quel'dorei Medley](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_cooking_10_draconicdelicacies.gif)Quel'dorei Medley](https://www.wowhead.com/item=242272/queldorei-medley) are equal

- Personal Food: [![Champion's Bento](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_misc_food_vendor_poundedricecake_1.gif)Champion's Bento](https://www.wowhead.com/item=242274/champions-bento)


In addition to food, you can also consume any tea (ex: [![Sanguithorn Tea](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/inv_12_profession_cooking_tea_purple.gif)Sanguithorn Tea](https://www.wowhead.com/item=242299/sanguithorn-tea)) for a movement speed increase that stacks with your food buff.
