## Preheat

### Coach, Former RWF Raider, & World of Warcraft Retail Expert

[Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/Preheat)[Discord](https://discord.gg/gd7jEPVcAT)[YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/@Preheat)[Bluesky](https://bsky.app/profile/preheat.bsky.social)[TikTok](https://www.tiktok.com/@preheatwow)[Author Page](https://www.wowhead.com/author/Preheat)

Preheat has raided at the highest level in the game for many years, was a founding member of LIMIT (now [Liquid](https://raider.io/guilds/us/illidan/Liquid)) and was an officer when they got World First. He also participated in the 2nd MDI back in Legion and was Rank 1 that season. He has also commentated multiple community events such as the [Keystone Masters](https://twitter.com/keystonemasters), numerous charity events, and the [Team Liquid](https://twitter.com/TeamLiquid) Race to World First. For the last 3 years, he has helped over 240 players improve their gameplay through [one-on-one coaching](https://metafy.gg/@preheat/sessions).

These days, Preheat focuses on content creation, casting events like the Race to World first, making guides, and coaching. Sharing what he knows and helping others improve is a passion of his and it is a big part of what drives him to keep playing the game.

Outside of World of Warcraft, Preheat has worked in the real world as a software trainer and has almost a decade of experience teaching people, writing documentation, and creating lesson plans for those looking to learn new, technical things.

For all things Evoker and Mage, you can find him in his [Discord](https://discord.gg/HzCKceX), on [Twitter](https://twitter.com/LimitPreheat), posting videos to [YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/c/preheat), and streaming on [Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat).

The **Wowhead Client** is a little application we use to keep our database up to date, and to provide you with some nifty extra functionality on the website!

It serves 2 main purposes:

1. It maintains a WoW addon called the **Wowhead Looter**, which collects data as you play the game!

2. It uploads the **collected data** to Wowhead in order to keep the database up-to-date!


You can also use it to keep track of your completed quests, recipes, mounts, companion pets, and titles!

So, what are you waiting for? [Download the client](https://www.wowhead.com/client) and get started.

# Devastation Evoker DPS Basics - Starter Guide for Midnight

[By Preheat](https://www.wowhead.com/author/Preheat)
Updated:
2026/02/24

LivePreheat[Live on Twitch!](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat) Offline on TwitchFollow: Follow [Discord](https://discord.gg/SmWHj8cQp7) [Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat) [Twitter](https://twitter.com/LimitPreheat) [YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/@Preheat)

Welcome to our Devastation Evoker starter guide, which covers everything you need to start playing a new character in Midnight. While our other guide pages go into more detail about individual aspects of Devastation Evoker gameplay, this simple starter guide covers all the basics in one place.

Visit the complete Devastation Evoker guide pages linked in the navigation on the left-hand side of the page for more in-depth information!

[Overview](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/overview-pve-dps) [BiS Gear](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/bis-gear) [Rotation](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/rotation-cooldowns-pve-dps) [Talent Builds](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/talent-builds-pve-dps) [Consumables](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/enchants-gems-pve-dps) [Stats](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/stat-priority-pve-dps) [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/large/inv_misc_groupneedmore.jpg)\\

## ~~~~ Talent Build for Devastation Evoker Beginners

The builds keep things simple without sacrificing performance. A lot of active abilities are removed in favor of passives. Scalecommander must use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) to be effective. If you would prefer not to use this ability, then Flameshaper might be for you.

If you're looking for more in-depth builds or explanations regarding talents choices, check out our Talent Builds guide:

[![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/medium/classicon_evoker_devastation.jpg)Devastation Evoker Talent Builds](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/talent-builds-pve-dps)

## ~~~~ Rotation Priority for Devastation Evoker Beginners

This is a simple example of the spell priorities that make up the Devastation Evoker rotation. More information about the rotation, as well as an explanation of important abilities and advanced tips can be found in the [Devastation Evoker Rotation Guide](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/rotation-cooldowns-pve-dps).

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| |     |     |
| --- | --- |
| ~~~~ | **Build and Spend:** [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_disintegrate.gif)Disintegrate](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=356995/disintegrate) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_pyre.gif)Pyre](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357211/pyre) spend [Essence](https://www.wowhead.com/resource/essence-19), primary resource for Evokers. [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_livingflame.gif)Living Flame](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=361469/living-flame) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_azurestrike.gif)Azure Strike](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=362969/azure-strike) are the filler spells that generate [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_essenceburst.gif)Essence Burst](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359618/essence-burst), a proc that allows you to use these abilities without spending Essence. Spend Essence and Essence Burst, then use filler spells while waiting. |

* * *

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| ~~~~ | **Use cooldowns frequently:** [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) (Scalecommander) are Devastation's main cooldown. Essence Burst is guaranteed from filler spells during this buff. Its duration can be extended by both [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_eternitysurge.gif)Eternity Surge](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=359073/eternity-surge) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_firebreath.gif)Fire Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357208/fire-breath) with [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/spell_nature_shamanrage.gif)Animosity](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375797/animosity). Given that both spells also come with their own short cooldowns, consider what is available when entering Dragonrage and to press them on cooldown during it. |

* * *

|     |     |
| --- | --- |
| ~~~~ | **Always be casting:** Try to maintain high uptime on targets. Constant use of damaging spells is the key to high damage in Raid and Mythic+. Use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) effectively to cast spells on the move. Try to fill "downtime" in your rotation with spells, even if its not completely optimal (Azure Strike for instance). Using _something_ is almost always better than being idle. Try to use cooldowns and Empowered spells early and often. | |

### Single Button Rotation Assistant

Devastation Evoker is a fairly simple and straightforward with its spell priority. It is a good fit for the Single Button Rotation Assistant tool. Devastation Evoker loses less from the Single Button Rotation Assistant tool than most classes, **meaning it is one of the best specs for it.** Keep in mind, the One-Button Rotation but it does not cast most dps cooldowns and utility abilities. It will also cancel channel spells and empower abilities if you mash the button too quickly.

When playing with the One-Button Rotation, try and cast your major dps cooldowns [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_dragonrage.gif)Dragonrage](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=375087/dragonrage) and [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_deepbreath.gif)Deep Breath](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=357210/deep-breath) as frequently as possible, and use [![](https://wow.zamimg.com/images/wow/icons/tiny/ability_evoker_hover.gif)Hover](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=358267/hover) whenever needed for movement. Hover allows you to continue to cast while moving.

## ~~~~ Best Stats for Devastation Evoker Beginners

The stat priority outlined below is the general priority for Devastation Evoker, and is well-suited for all levels of play. **Critical Strike** is generally your base stat. **Haste** and **Mastery** are fairly close.

For more details, check our [Devastation Evoker Stats guide](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/classes/evoker/devastation/stat-priority-pve-dps).

1. Main Stat (Item Level)

2. Critical Strike

3. Haste = Mastery

4. Versatility

## Preheat

### Coach, Former RWF Raider, & World of Warcraft Retail Expert

[Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/Preheat)[Discord](https://discord.gg/gd7jEPVcAT)[YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/@Preheat)[Bluesky](https://bsky.app/profile/preheat.bsky.social)[TikTok](https://www.tiktok.com/@preheatwow)[Author Page](https://www.wowhead.com/author/Preheat)

Preheat has raided at the highest level in the game for many years, was a founding member of LIMIT (now [Liquid](https://raider.io/guilds/us/illidan/Liquid)) and was an officer when they got World First. He also participated in the 2nd MDI back in Legion and was Rank 1 that season. He has also commentated multiple community events such as the [Keystone Masters](https://twitter.com/keystonemasters), numerous charity events, and the [Team Liquid](https://twitter.com/TeamLiquid) Race to World First. For the last 3 years, he has helped over 240 players improve their gameplay through [one-on-one coaching](https://metafy.gg/@preheat/sessions).

These days, Preheat focuses on content creation, casting events like the Race to World first, making guides, and coaching. Sharing what he knows and helping others improve is a passion of his and it is a big part of what drives him to keep playing the game.

Outside of World of Warcraft, Preheat has worked in the real world as a software trainer and has almost a decade of experience teaching people, writing documentation, and creating lesson plans for those looking to learn new, technical things.

For all things Evoker and Mage, you can find him in his [Discord](https://discord.gg/HzCKceX), on [Twitter](https://twitter.com/LimitPreheat), posting videos to [YouTube](https://www.youtube.com/c/preheat), and streaming on [Twitch](https://www.twitch.tv/preheat).
